 CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_velocity_stats_last_updated ON velocity_stats(last_updated);\n299\t\n300\t    -- Migration Lock (bf-only table for coordinating workspace migrations)\n301\t    -- migration_lock (\n304\t        id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),\n305\t        locked_by   TEXT NOT NULL,\n306\t        locked_at   DATETIME NOT NULL,\n307\t        expires_at  DATETIME NOT NULL\n308\t    );\n309\t\";\n310\t\n311\t/// Split a SQL script into individual statements, respecting string literals,\n312\t/// quoted identifiers, and comments.\n313\t///\n314\t/// A naive `split(';')` breaks when SQL string literals contain semicolons\n315\t/// (e.g., `INSERT INTO t(v) VALUES('a;b')`). This function uses a small state\n316\t/// machine to track whether the current position is inside:\n317\t/// - A single-quoted string literal (`'...'`, with `''` as escape)\n318\t/// - A double-quoted identifier (`\"...\"`, with `\"\"` as escape)\n319\t/// - A line comment (`-- ...`)\n320\t/// - A block comment (`/* ... */`)\n321\t///\n322\t/// Only semicolons at the top level (outside all of the above) are treated as\n323\t/// statement terminators.\n324\tfn split_sql_statements(sql: &str) -> Vec<&str> {\n325\t    let bytes = sql.as_bytes();\n326\t    let len = bytes.len();\n327\t    let mut stmts = Vec::new();\n328\t    let mut start = 0; // byte offset where the current statement begins\n329\t    let mut i = 0;\n330\t\n331\t    // State flags — at most one is true at a time.\n332\t    let mut in_single_quote = false;\n333\t    let mut in_double_quote = false;\n334\t    let mut in_line_comment = false;\n335\t    let mut in_block_comment = false;\n336\t\n337\t    while i < len {\n338\t        let b = bytes[i];\n339\t\n340\t        // --- Line comment state ---\n341\t        if in_line_comment {\n342\t            if b == b'\\n' {\n343\t                in_line_comment = false;\n344\t            }\n345\t            i += 1;\n346\t            continue;\n347\t        }\n348\t\n349\t        // --- Block comment state ---\n350\t        if in_block_comment {\n351\t            if b == b'*' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'/' {\n352\t                in_block_comment = false;\n353\t                i += 2;\n354\t            } else {\n355\t                i += 1;\n356\t            }\n357\t            continue;\n358\t        }\n359\t\n360\t        // --- Single-quoted string state ---\n361\t        if in_single_quote {\n362\t            if b == b'\\'' {\n363\t                // '' is an escaped quote inside a string literal\n364\t                if i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'\\'' {\n365\t                    i += 2;\n366\t                } else {\n367\t                    in_single_quote = false;\n368\t                    i += 1;\n369\t                }\n370\t            } else {\n371\t                i += 1;\n372\t            }\n373\t            continue;\n374\t        }\n375\t\n376\t        // --- Double-quoted identifier state ---\n377\t        if in_double_quote {\n378\t            if b == b'\"' {\n379\t                if i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'\"' {\n380\t                    i += 2;\n381\t                } else {\n382\t                    in_double_quote = false;\n383\t                    i += 1;\n384\t                }\n385\t            } else {\n386\t                i += 1;\n387\t            }\n388\t            continue;\n389\t        }\n390\t\n391\t        // --- Top-level parsing ---\n392\t        if b == b'\\'' {\n393\t            in_single_quote = true;\n394\t            i += 1;\n395\t        } else if b == b'\"' {\n396\t            in_double_quote = true;\n397\t            i += 1;\n398\t        } else if b == b'-' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'-' {\n399\t            in_line_comment = true;\n400\t            i += 2;\n401\t        } else if b == b'/' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'*' {\n402\t            in_block_comment = true;\n403\t            i += 2;\n404\t        } else if b == b';' {\n405\t            // Statement terminator at top level.\n406\t            let stmt = &sql[start..i];\n407\t            if !stmt.trim().is_empty() {\n408\t                stmts.push(stmt.trim());\n409\t            }\n410\t            start = i + 1;\n411\t            i += 1;\n412\t        } else {\n413\t            i += 1;\n414\t        }\n415\t    }\n416\t\n417\t    // Trailing statement without a final semicolon.\n418\t    if start < len {\n419\t        let events(created_at);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_actor ON events(actor) WHERE actor != '';\n\n    -- Config (Runtime)\n    -- NOTE: Avoid PRIMARY KEY/UNIQUE constraints here because the current\n    -- storage engine does not reliably maintain unique autoindexes.\n    -- Application code enforces key replacement via DELETE + INSERT.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS config (\n        key TEXT NOT NULL,\n        value TEXT NOT NULL\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_config_key ON config(key);\n\n    -- Metadata\n    -- Same rationale as config: keep it as key-value with CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS export_hashes (\n        issue_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        content_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n        exported_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n        FOREIGN KEY (issue_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n\n    -- Blocked Issues Cache (Materialized view)\n    -- Rebuilt on dependency or status changes.\n    -- `blocked_by` stores a JSON CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n        FOREIGN KEY (issue_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_blocked_cache_blocked_at ON blocked_issues_cache(blocked_at);\n\n    -- Child Counters (for hierarchical IDs like bd-abc.1, bd-abc.2)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS child_counters (\n        parent_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        last_child INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        FOREIGN KEY (parent_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n\n    -- Recovery Sessions (for anomaly/recovery audit trail)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recovery_sessions (\n        correlation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        started_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n        completed_at TEXT,\n        final_outcome TEXT,\n        session_type TEXT NOT NULL,\n        trigger_reason TEXT,\n        workspace_path TEXT,\n        summary TEXT\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_recovery_sessions_started ON recovery_sessions(started_at);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS anomaly/recovery event tracking)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS anomaly_audit (\n        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n        correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n        timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,\n        event_type TEXT NOT NULL,\n        anomaly_type TEXT,\n        recovery_strategy TEXT,\n        outcome TEXT,\n        evidence TEXT,\n        metadata TEXT,\n        created_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP),\n        FOREIGN KEY (correlation_id) REFERENCES recovery_sessions(correlation_id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anomaly_audit_correlation ON anomaly_audit(correlation_id);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anomaly_audit_timestamp ON anomaly_audit(timestamp);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anomaly_audit_event_type ON anomaly_audit(event_type);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anomaly_audit_anomaly_type ON anomaly_audit(anomaly_type);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anomaly_audit_outcome ON anomaly_audit(outcome);\n\n    -- Critical Path Cache (for impact-weighted claim scoring)\n    -- Computed from dependency graph; lower float = more critical (0 = on critical path)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS critical_path_cache (\n        bead_id     TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        epic_id     TEXT REFERENCES issues(id),\n        es          INTEGER NOT NULL,   -- earliest start (hops from root)\n        ls          INTEGER NOT NULL,   -- latest start\n        float       INTEGER NOT NULL,   -- ls - es; 0 = critical path\n        updated_at  DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n        FOREIGN KEY (bead_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n\n    -- Bead Annotations (bf-only table, never touched by br)\n    -- Stores arbitrary key-value metadata per bead.\n    -- IMPORTANT: This is a SEPARATE table (not a column on issues) because br's\n    -- issues_column_order_matches() check triggers rebuild_issues_table() when\n    -- the column count differs, which would silently destroy any extra column.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bead_annotations (\n        bead_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n        key     TEXT NOT NULL,\n        value   TEXT NOT NULL,\n        PRIMARY KEY (bead_id, key)\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bead_annotations_key_value\n        ON bead_annotations (key, value);\n\n    -- Worker Sessions (bf-only table for multi-workspace claiming)\n    -- Tracks worker metadata (model, harness, version) for each claim operation.\n    -- Used by velocity-aware scoring and audit trails.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS worker_sessions (\n        worker_id        TEXT NOT NULL,\n        model            TEXT,\n        harness          TEXT,\n        harness_version Aggregated statistics per (model, harness, issue_type) tuple.\n    -- Updated on bead close to inform velocity-aware scoring.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS velocity_stats (\n        model           TEXT NOT NULL,\n        harness         TEXT NOT NULL,\n        issue_type      TEXT NOT NULL,\n        sample_count    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,\n        p50_seconds     INTEGER,\n        p90_seconds     INTEGER,\n        avg_seconds     REAL,\n        last_updated    DATETIME,\n        PRIMARY KEY (model, harness, issue_type)\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_velocity_stats_last_updated ON velocity_stats(last_updated);\n\n    -- Migration Lock (bf-only table for coordinating workspace migrations)\n    -- Singleton table that prevents new claims during migration operations.\n    -- Checked by bf claim at the start of each BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS migration_lock (\n        id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),\n        locked_by   TEXT NOT NULL,\n        locked_at   DATETIME NOT NULL,\n        expires_at  DATETIME NOT NULL\n    );\n\";\n\n/// Split a SQL script into individual statements, respecting string literals,\n/// quoted identifiers, and comments.\n///\n/// A naive `split(';')` breaks when SQL string literals contain semicolons\n/// (e.g., `INSERT INTO t(v) VALUES('a;b')`). This function uses a small state\n/// machine to track whether the current position is inside:\n/// - A single-quoted string literal (`'...'`, with `''` as escape)\n/// - A double-quoted identifier (`\"...\"`, with `\"\"` as escape)\n/// - A line comment (`-- ...`)\n/// - A block comment (`/* ... */`)\n///\n/// Only semicolons at the top level (outside all of the above) are treated as\n/// statement terminators.\nfn split_sql_statements(sql: &str) -> Vec<&str> {\n    let bytes = sql.as_bytes();\n    let len = bytes.len();\n    let mut stmts = Vec::new();\n    let mut start = 0; // byte offset where the current statement begins\n    let mut i = 0;\n\n    // State flags — at most one is true at a time.\n    let mut in_single_quote = false;\n    let mut in_double_quote = false;\n    let mut in_line_comment = false;\n    let mut in_block_comment = false;\n\n    while i < len {\n        let b = bytes[i];\n\n        // --- Line comment state ---\n        if in_line_comment {\n            if b == b'\\n' {\n                in_line_comment = false;\n            }\n            i += 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // --- Block comment state ---\n        if in_block_comment {\n            if b == b'*' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'/' {\n                in_block_comment = false;\n                i += 2;\n            } else {\n                i += 1;\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // --- Single-quoted string state ---\n        if in_single_quote {\n            if b == b'\\'' {\n                // '' is an escaped quote inside a string literal\n                if i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'\\'' {\n                    i += 2;\n                } else {\n                    in_single_quote = false;\n                    i += 1;\n                }\n            } else {\n                i += 1;\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // --- Double-quoted identifier state ---\n        if in_double_quote {\n            if b == b'\"' {\n                if i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'\"' {\n                    i += 2;\n                } else {\n                    in_double_quote = false;\n                    i += 1;\n                }\n            } else {\n                i += 1;\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // --- Top-level parsing ---\n        if b == b'\\'' {\n            in_single_quote = true;\n            i += 1;\n        } else if b == b'\"' {\n            in_double_quote = true;\n            i += 1;\n        } else if b == b'-' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'-' {\n            in_line_comment = true;\n            i += 2;\n        } else if b == b'/' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'*' {\n            in_block_comment = true;\n            i += 2;\n        } else if b == b';' {\n            // Statement terminator at top level.\n            let stmt = &sql[start..i];\n            if !stmt.trim().is_empty() {\n                stmts.push(stmt.trim());\n            }\n            start = i + 1;\n            i += 1;\n        } else {\n            i += 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Trailing statement without a final semicolon.\n    if start < len {\n        let stmt = &sql[start..len];\n        if !stmt.trim().is_empty() {\n            stmts.push(stmt.trim());\n        }\n    }\n\n    stmts\n}\n\n/// Execute multiple SQL statements separated by semicolons.\nfn execute_batch(conn: &Connection, sql: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n    conn.execute_batch(sql)?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\npub fn apply_schema(conn: &Connection) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n    execute_batch(conn, SCHEMA_SQL)?;\n    apply_migrations(conn)?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Apply database migrations for schema changes.\n/// This handles adding new columns to existing tables.\nfn apply_migrations(conn: &Connection) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n    // Migration 1: Update critical_path_cache schema if it has the old format\n    // Old format: bead_id, float (REAL), updated_at\n    // New format: bead_id, epic_id, es (INTEGER), ls (INTEGER), float (INTEGER), updated_at\n    let has_es: bool = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('critical_path_cache') WHERE name='es'\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0).map(|n| n > 0),\n    ).unwrap_or(false);\n\n    if !has_es {\n        // Table has old schema - recreate with new schema\n        conn.execute_batch(\n            \"BEGIN;\n             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS critical_path_cache_new (\n                 bead_id     TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n                 epic_id     TEXT,\n                 es          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n                 ls          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n                 float       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n                 updated_at  DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                 FOREIGN KEY (bead_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n             );\n             INSERT INTO critical_path_cache_new (bead_id, float, updated_at)\n                 SELECT bead_id, CAST(float AS INTEGER), updated_at FROM critical_path_cache;\n             DROP TABLE critical_path_cache;\n             ALTER TABLE critical_path_cache_new RENAME TO critical_path_cache;\n             COMMIT;\"\n        )?;\n    }\n\n    // Create indexes for critical_path_cache (idempotent)\n    let _ = conn.execute_batch(\n        \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_critical_path_cache_epic ON critical_path_cache(epic_id);\n         CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_critical_path_cache_float ON critical_path_cache(float);\"\n    );\n\n    // Migration 2: Add workspace_path to worker_sessions if missing\n    // Older databases may not have this column; br never reads worker_sessions so this is safe.\n    let has_workspace_path: bool = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('worker_sessions') WHERE name='workspace_path'\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0).map(|n| n > 0),\n    ).unwrap_or(false);\n\n    if !has_workspace_path {\n        conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE worker_sessions ADD COLUMN workspace_path TEXT\", [])?;\n    }\n\n    // Migration 3: Add closed_at and duration_seconds to worker_sessions for velocity tracking\n    let has_closed_at: bool = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Ok(())\n}\n\npub fn ensure_wal_mode(conn: &Connection) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n    // Use execute_batch so PRAGMAs that return rows don't cause errors\n    conn.execute_batch(\n        \"PRAGMA Ok(workspace_path.to_path_buf())\n496\t    } else {\n497\t        use anyhow::bail;\n498\t        bail!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace_path)\n499\t    }\n500\t}\n501\t\n502\t/// Find directories.\n506\tpub fn find_workspaces(start_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>> {\n507\t\n508\t    let mut workspaces = Vec::new();\n509\t\n510\t    // Start from the given path and search anyhow::Result;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Duration, Utc};\nuse rusqlite::{params, Connection};\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\n/// Worker metadata for tracking which model/harness claimed a bead\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]\npub struct WorkerMetadata {\n    pub worker_id: String,\n    pub model: Option<String>,\n    pub harness: Option<String>,\n    pub harness_version: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// Result of a claim operation\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct ClaimResult {\n    pub bead_id: String,\n    pub reclaimed: usize,\n    pub workspace_path: Option<PathBuf>,\n}\n\n/// Score for cross-workspace candidate comparison.\n///\n/// Higher scores are better. Ordered by:\n/// 1. downstream_impact (more blocking = higher priority)\n/// 2. negative critical_float (lower float = more critical)\n/// 3. negative priority (lower number = higher priority)\n/// 4. negative created timestamp (older = higher priority/FIFO)\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct Score {\n    pub downstream_impact: i64,\n    pub critical_float: i64,\n    pub priority: i32,\n    pub created_at_ts: i64,\n}\n\nimpl Score {\n    /// Create a new score from candidate fields.\n    pub fn new(downstream_impact: i64, critical_float: i64, priority: i32, created_at_ts: i64) -> Self {\n        Self { downstream_impact, critical_float, priority, created_at_ts }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl PartialOrd for Score {\n    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<std::cmp::Ordering> {\n        Some(self.cmp(other))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Ord for Score {\n    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> std::cmp::Ordering {\n        // downstream_impact: DESC (higher is better)\n        match other.downstream_impact.cmp(&self.downstream_impact) {\n            std::cmp::Ordering::Equal => {}\n            ord => return ord,\n        }\n        // critical_float: ASC (lower is better)\n        match self.critical_float.cmp(&other.critical_float) {\n            std::cmp::Ordering::Equal => {}\n            ord => return ord,\n        }\n        // priority: ASC (lower number is better)\n        match self.priority.cmp(&other.priority) {\n            std::cmp::Ordering::Equal => {}\n            ord => return ord,\n        }\n        // created_at_ts: ASC (older is better/FIFO)\n        self.created_at_ts.cmp(&other.created_at_ts)\n    }\n}\n\n/// A bead with its score for ready/claim operations\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]\npub struct ScoredBead {\n    pub id: String,\n    pub title: String,\n    pub status: String,\n    pub priority: i32,\n    pub downstream_impact: i64,\n    pub critical_float: f64,\n    pub created_at: String,\n}\n\n/// Atomically claim a bead for a worker.\n///\n/// This function performs the following in a single IMMEDIATE transaction:\n/// 1. Reclaim stale in_progress beads (older than claim_ttl_minutes) back to open\n/// 2. Select candidates with downstream_impact + critical_float scoring\n/// 3. Update the winner to in_progress with assignee=worker\n/// 4. Insert an event\n/// 5. Mark the bead as dirty\n/// 6. Commit\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use (must be an IMMEDIATE transaction)\n/// * `worker` - The worker ID claiming the bead\n/// * `claim_ttl_minutes` - TTL in minutes after which in_progress beads are reclaimed\n/// * `worker_metadata` - Optional worker metadata (model, harness, version)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Some(claim_result))` - A bead was claimed\n/// * `Ok(None)` - No beads available to claim\n/// * `Err(e)` - Transaction error\npub fn claim(\n    tx: &Connection,\n    worker: &str,\n    claim_ttl_minutes: i64,\n    now: DateTime<Utc>,\n    worker_metadata: Option<&WorkerMetadata>,\n) -> Result<Option<ClaimResult>> {\n\n    // Step 0: Check migration_lock - return NONE if migration is in progress\n    let lock_count: i64 = tx.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM migration_lock WHERE expires_at > ?1\",\n        params![now.to_rfc3339()],\n        |row| row.get(0),\n    ).unwrap_or(0);\n    if lock_count > 0 {\n        // Migration in progress - return None gracefully\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n\n    // Step 1: Reclaim stale in_progress beads\n    let stale_cutoff = now - Duration::minutes(claim_ttl_minutes);\n    let reclaimed = tx.execute(\n        \"UPDATE issues\n         SET status = 'open', assignee = NULL, updated_at = ?\n         WHERE status = 'in_progress'\n           AND updated_at < ?\",\n        params![now.to_rfc3339(), stale_cutoff.to_rfc3339()],\n    )?;\n\n    // Step 2: Find candidate beads with impact scoring\n    // Score = downstream_impact + critical_path_bonus\n    // downstream_impact = count of beads blocked by this one\n    // critical_path_bonus = 1000.0 / (float + 1) where float is from critical_path_cache\n    // Zero-float beads get bonus = 1000, float-5 beads get ~167, non-critical beads get ~1\n    // Velocity-aware scoring: prefer beads with lower expected duration\n    // score = impact / expected_seconds (with fallback to standard scoring)\n    let (model, harness) = if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n        (meta.model.clone(), meta.harness.clone())\n    } else {\n        (None, None)\n    };\n\n    // Velocity-aware claim: get all candidates and score in Rust\n    if model.is_some() && harness.is_some() {\n        let m = model.as_ref().map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or(\"\");\n        let h = harness.as_ref().map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or(\"\");\n\n        let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n            \"SELECT i.id, i.issue_type,\n                    COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n                    1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n                    i.priority, i.created_at\n             FROM issues i\n             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n               AND i.ephemeral = 0\n               AND i.pinned = 0\n               AND i.is_template = 0\n               AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n               AND NOT EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n                   INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n                   WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n               )\n             GROUP BY i.id\",\n        )?;\n\n        let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n\n        // Score all candidates and pick the best\n        let mut best_candidate: Option<(String, f64)> = None;\n\n        while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n            let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n            let issue_type: String = row.get(1)?;\n            let downstream_impact: i64 = row.get(2)?;\n            let critical_path_bonus: f64 = row.get(3)?;\n            let _priority: i32 = row.get(4)?;\n            let _created_at: String = row.get(5)?;\n\n            // Get expected seconds from velocity stats\n            let expected_seconds = crate::velocity::get_expected_seconds(tx, m, h, &issue_type)?\n                .unwrap_or(60); // Default to 60 seconds if no data\n\n            // Velocity-aware score: impact per second\n            // Use expected_seconds as denominator to prefer faster completions\n            let velocity_score = (downstream_impact as f64) / (expected_seconds as f64);\n\n            // Combine with critical path bonus\n            let score = velocity_score + critical_path_bonus;\n\n            match &best_candidate {\n                None => best_candidate = Some((bead_id, score)),\n                Some((_, best_score)) if score > *best_score => {\n                    best_candidate = Some((bead_id, score));\n                }\n                _ => {}\n            }\n        }\n\n        if let Some((bead_id, _)) = best_candidate {\n            // Step 3: Update the winner to in_progress with a race condition check\n            let rows_affected = tx.execute(\n                \"UPDATE issues\n                 SET status = 'in_progress', assignee = ?, updated_at = ?\n                 WHERE id = ? AND status = 'open'\",\n                params![worker, now.to_rfc3339(), &bead_id],\n            )?;\n\n            if rows_affected == 0 {\n                return Ok(None);\n            }\n\n            // Step 4: Record worker session if metadata provided\n            if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n                tx.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO worker_sessions (worker_id, model, harness, harness_version, bead_id, workspace_path)\n                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)\",\n                    params![\n                        &meta.worker_id,\n                        meta.model.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness_version.as_deref(),\n                        &bead_id,\n                        \"\",\n                    ],\n                )?;\n            }\n\n            // Step 5: Insert event with worker metadata in comment field\n            let metadata_json = worker_metadata.and_then(|m| serde_json::to_string(m).ok());\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO events (issue_id, event_type, actor, new_value, comment, created_at)\n                 VALUES (?, 'claimed', ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, worker, worker, metadata_json, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            // Step 6: Mark as dirty\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO dirty_issues (issue_id, marked_at)\n                 VALUES (?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            return Ok(Some(ClaimResult {\n                bead_id,\n                reclaimed,\n                workspace_path: None,\n            }));\n        }\n\n        Ok(None)\n    } else {\n        // Standard scoring without velocity data (original SQL-based scoring)\n        let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n            \"SELECT i.id, i.issue_type,\n                    COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n                    1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n                    i.priority\n             FROM issues i\n             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n               AND i.ephemeral = 0\n               AND i.pinned = 0\n               AND i.is_template = 0\n               AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n               AND NOT EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n                   INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n                   WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n               )\n             GROUP BY i.id\n             ORDER BY\n                 downstream_impact DESC,\n                 critical_path_bonus DESC,\n                 i.priority ASC,\n                 i.created_at ASC\n             LIMIT 1\",\n        )?;\n\n        let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n\n        if let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n            let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n\n            // Step 3: Update the winner to in_progress with a race condition check\n            // The WHERE status = 'open' condition ensures we only claim if still open\n            let rows_affected = tx.execute(\n                \"UPDATE issues\n                 SET status = 'in_progress', assignee = ?, updated_at = ?\n                 WHERE id = ? AND status = 'open'\",\n                params![worker, now.to_rfc3339(), &bead_id],\n            )?;\n\n            // If no rows were affected, another worker claimed this bead first\n            if rows_affected == 0 {\n                return Ok(None);\n            }\n\n            // Step 4: Record worker session if metadata provided\n            if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n                tx.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO worker_sessions (worker_id, model, harness, harness_version, bead_id, workspace_path)\n                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)\",\n                    params![\n                        &meta.worker_id,\n                        meta.model.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness_version.as_deref(),\n                        &bead_id,\n                        \"\", // workspace_path not available in transaction context\n                    ],\n                )?;\n            }\n\n            // Step 5: Insert event with worker metadata in comment field\n            let metadata_json = worker_metadata.and_then(|m| serde_json::to_string(m).ok());\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO events (issue_id, event_type, actor, new_value, comment, created_at)\n                 VALUES (?, 'claimed', ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, worker, worker, metadata_json, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            // Step 6: Mark as dirty\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO dirty_issues (issue_id, marked_at)\n                 VALUES (?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            Ok(Some(ClaimResult {\n                bead_id,\n                reclaimed,\n                workspace_path: None,\n            }))\n        } else {\n            Ok(None)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Get ready candidates using the same scoring logic as claim().\n///\n/// This returns a list of beads that would be considered for claiming,\n/// ordered by the same scoring formula:\n/// - downstream_impact DESC (more blocking = higher priority)\n/// - critical_path_bonus DESC (1000.0/(float+1), higher bonus = more critical)\n/// - priority ASC (0=Critical, 4=Backlog)\n/// - created_at ASC (FIFO tiebreaker)\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n/// * `limit` - Maximum number of candidates to return\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Vec<ScoredBead>)` - List of scored bead candidates\npub fn get_ready_candidates(tx: &Connection, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<ScoredBead>> {\n    let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n        \"SELECT i.id, i.title, i.status, i.priority,\n                COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n                1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n                i.created_at\n         FROM issues i\n         LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n         LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n         WHERE i.status = 'open'\n           AND i.ephemeral = 0\n           AND i.pinned = 0\n           AND i.is_template = 0\n           AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n           AND NOT EXISTS (\n               SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n               INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n               WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n               AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n               AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n           )\n         GROUP BY i.id\n         ORDER BY\n             downstream_impact DESC,\n             critical_path_bonus DESC,\n             i.priority ASC,\n             i.created_at ASC\n         LIMIT ?1\",\n    )?;\n\n    let mut rows = stmt.query(params![limit as i64])?;\n    let mut candidates = Vec::new();\n\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n        candidates.push(ScoredBead {\n            id: row.get(0)?,\n            title: row.get(1)?,\n            status: row.get(2)?,\n            priority: row.get(3)?,\n            downstream_impact: row.get(4)?,\n            critical_float: row.get(5)?,\n            created_at: row.get(6)?,\n        });\n    }\n\n    Ok(candidates)\n}\n\n/// Claim from the highest-priority bead across multiple workspaces.\n///\n/// Scores each workspace's top candidate, picks the global winner,\n/// and claims from that workspace.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_paths` - Slice of workspace directory paths\n/// * `worker` - The worker ID claiming the bead\n/// * `claim_ttl_minutes` - TTL in minutes after which in_progress beads are reclaimed\n/// * `worker_metadata` - Optional worker metadata (model, harness, version)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Some(claim_result))` - A bead was claimed (with workspace_path set)\n/// * `Ok(None)` - No beads available to claim in any workspace\n/// * `Err(e)` - Transaction error\npub fn claim_any(\n    workspace_paths: &[PathBuf],\n    worker: &str,\n    claim_ttl_minutes: i64,\n    worker_metadata: Option<&WorkerMetadata>,\n) -> Result<Option<ClaimResult>> {\n    use crate::config::load_metadata;\n    use crate::storage::Storage;\n\n    // Score across all workspaces\n    let mut best: Option<(Score, usize)> = None;\n    for (idx, workspace_path) in workspace_paths.iter().enumerate() {\n        let beads_dir = get_beads_dir(workspace_path)?;\n        let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n        let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n        // Open each workspace's SQLite\n        match Storage::open(&db_path) {\n            Ok(storage) => {\n                if let Some(score) = storage.top_candidate_score()? {\n                    if best.as_ref().map(|(b, _)| score > *b).unwrap_or(true) {\n                        best = Some((score, idx));\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Err(_) => {\n                // Skip workspaces that can't be opened (e.g., no .beads directory)\n                continue;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    match best {\n        None => Ok(None),\n        Some((_, workspace_idx)) => {\n            let workspace_path = &workspace_paths[workspace_idx];\n            let beads_dir = get_beads_dir(workspace_path)?;\n            let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n            let now = Utc::now();\n            match storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                claim(tx, worker, claim_ttl_minutes, now, worker_metadata)\n            })? {\n                Some(mut result) => {\n                    result.workspace_path = Some(workspace_path.clone());\n                    Ok(Some(result))\n                }\n                None => Ok(None),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Get the .beads directory from a workspace path.\n///\n/// If the workspace path itself contains a .beads directory, use it.\n/// Otherwise, assume the path IS the .beads directory.\nfn get_beads_dir(workspace_path: &Path) -> Result<std::path::PathBuf> {\n    let beads_dir = workspace_path.join(\".beads\");\n    if beads_dir.is_dir() {\n        Ok(beads_dir)\n    } else if workspace_path.ends_with(\".beads\") {\n        Ok(workspace_path.to_path_buf())\n    } else {\n        use anyhow::bail;\n        bail!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace_path)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Find all bead workspace directories starting from a search path.\n///\n/// Searches for directories containing a .beads subdirectory.\n/// Searches upward from the start path through parent directories.\npub fn find_workspaces(start_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>> {\n\n    let mut workspaces = Vec::new();\n\n    // Start from the given path and search upward\n    let mut current = start_path.to_path_buf();\n    loop {\n        let beads_dir = current.join(\".beads\");\n        if beads_dir.is_dir() {\n            // Found a workspace - add the parent directory\n            workspaces.push(current.clone());\n        }\n\n        // Move to parent directory\n        if !current.pop() {\n            // Reached the root, stop searching\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(workspaces)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::storage::Storage;\n    use crate::model::{Issue, Status};\n\n    fn setup_test_db() -> (tempfile::NamedTempFile, Storage) {\n        let temp_file = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new().unwrap();\n        let storage = Storage::open(temp_file.path()).unwrap();\n        (temp_file, storage)\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_claim_basic() {\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create an open bead\n        let issue = Issue::new(\"bf-test1\".to_string(), \"Test bead\".to_string(), \".\".to_string());\n        storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n\n        // Claim it\n        let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n            claim(tx, \"worker1\", 30, Utc::now(), None)\n        }).unwrap();\n\n        assert!(result.is_some());\n        let claim_result = result.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(claim_result.bead_id, \"bf-test1\");\n        assert_eq!(claim_result.reclaimed, 0);\n\n        // Verify the bead is now in_progress\n        let updated = storage.get_issue(\"bf-test1\").unwrap().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(updated.status, Status::InProgress);\n        assert_eq!(updated.assignee.as_ref().unwrap(), \"worker1\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_claim_no_candidates() {\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // No beads available\n        let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n            claim(tx, \"worker1\", 30, Utc::now(), None)\n        }).unwrap();\n\n        assert!(result.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_claim_reclaims_stale() {\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create an in_progress bead with old updated_at\n        let mut issue = Issue::new(\"bf-stale\".to_string(), \"Stale bead\".to_string(), \".\".to_string());\n        issue.status = Status::InProgress;\n        issue.assignee = Some(\"worker_old\".to_string());\n        issue.updated_at = Utc::now() - Duration::minutes(60);\n        storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n\n        // Create an open bead\n        let issue2 = Issue::new(\"bf-open\".to_string(), \"Open bead\".to_string(), \".\".to_string());\n        storage.create_issue(&issue2).unwrap();\n\n        // Claim with 30 min TTL - should reclaim the stale one\n        let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n            claim(tx, \"worker_new\", 30, Utc::now(), None)\n        }).unwrap();\n\n        assert!(result.is_some());\n        let claim_result = result.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(claim_result.reclaimed, 1);\n\n        // The open bead should be claimed (not the stale one, since it was reclaimed to open)\n        // After reclaim, the stale bead is open again, so it could be claimed too\n        // But the open bead has priority by created_at order\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_concurrent_claim_no_double_claim() {\n        use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\n        use std::thread;\n\n        let (_temp, storage) = setup_test_db();\n        let storage = Arc::new(storage);\n\n        // Create 20 open beads\n        for i in 0..20 {\n            let issue = Issue::new(format!(\"bf-{:0>4}\", i), format!(\"Test bead {}\", i), \".\".to_string());\n            storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n        }\n\n        // Spawn 20 workers trying to claim concurrently\n        let claimed_beads: Arc<Mutex<Vec<String>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));\n        let mut handles = vec![];\n\n        for worker_id in 0..20 {\n            let storage_clone = Arc::clone(&storage);\n            let claimed_clone = Arc::clone(&claimed_beads);\n\n            let handle = thread::spawn(move || {\n                let result = storage_clone.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                    claim(tx, &format!(\"worker-{}\", worker_id), 30, Utc::now(), None)\n                }).unwrap();\n\n                if let Some(claim_result) = result {\n                    let mut claimed = claimed_clone.lock().unwrap();\n                    claimed.push(claim_result.bead_id);\n                }\n            });\n\n            handles.push(handle);\n        }\n\n        // Wait for all workers to complete\n        for handle in handles {\n            handle.join().unwrap();\n        }\n\n        let claimed = claimed_beads.lock().unwrap();\n\n        // All 20 beads should be claimed exactly once\n        assert_eq!(claimed.len(), 20, \"Expected 20 unique claims, got {}\", claimed.len());\n\n        // No duplicates allowed\n        let mut unique_beads = claimed.clone();\n        unique_beads.sort();\n        unique_beads.dedup();\n        assert_eq!(unique_beads.len(), 20, \"Found duplicate claims: {:?}\", claimed);\n    }\n}\n","numLines":659,"startLine":1,"totalLines":659}}}

Worker performance tracking and velocity-aware claim scoring.\n//!\n//! This module tracks how long workers take to complete beads based on their\n//! (model, harness, issue_type) configuration and uses this data to inform\n//! claim scoring.\n//!\n//! ## Data Flow\n//!\n//! 1. When a bead is claimed, `worker_sessions` row is created (in claim.rs)\n//! 2. When a bead is closed, the session is updated with `closed_at` and `duration_seconds`\n//! 3. Velocity stats are recomputed from the last 50 sessions per (model, harness, issue_type)\n//! 4. Claim scoring uses expected_seconds from velocity_stats: score = impact / expected_seconds\n\nuse anyhow::Result;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse rusqlite::{params, Connection};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\n/// Velocity statistics for a (model, harness, issue_type) tuple.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct VelocityStats {\n    pub model: String,\n    pub harness: String,\n    pub issue_type: String,\n    pub sample_count: i64,\n    pub p50_seconds: Option<i64>,\n    pub p90_seconds: Option<i64>,\n    pub avg_seconds: Option<f64>,\n    pub last_updated: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// Worker session record from worker_sessions table.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct WorkerSession {\n    pub worker_id: String,\n    pub model: Option<String>,\n    pub harness: Option<String>,\n    pub harness_version: Option<String>,\n    pub claimed_at: String,\n    pub bead_id: Option<String>,\n    pub workspace_path: String,\n    pub closed_at: Option<String>,\n    pub duration_seconds: Option<i64>,\n}\n\n/// Update a worker session with close time and duration.\n///\n/// Called when a bead is closed. Finds the most recent claim session for the bead\n/// and updates it with closed_at and duration_seconds.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n/// * `bead_id` - The bead that was closed\n/// * `closed_at` - When the bead was closed\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(true)` if a session was found and updated\n/// * `Ok(false)` if no matching session was found\npub fn update_session_on_close(\n    tx: &Connection,\n    bead_id: &str,\n    closed_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n) -> Result<bool> {\n    // Find the most recent session for this bead\n    let (claimed_at_str, model, harness, issue_type): (String, Option<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>) =\n        tx.query_row(\n            \"SELECT ws.claimed_at, ws.model, ws.harness, i.issue_type\n             FROM worker_sessions ws\n             INNER JOIN issues i ON i.id = ws.bead_id\n             WHERE ws.bead_id = ?1\n             ORDER BY ws.claimed_at DESC\n             LIMIT 1\",\n            params![bead_id],\n            |row| {\n                let claimed_at: String = row.get(0)?;\n                let model: Option<String> = row.get(1)?;\n                let harness: Option<String> = row.get(2)?;\n                let issue_type: Option<String> = row.get(3)?;\n                Ok((claimed_at, model, harness, issue_type))\n            },\n        )?;\n\n    // Parse claimed_at and calculate duration\n    let claimed_at = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&claimed_at_str)\n        .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Invalid claimed_at format: {}\", e))?\n        .with_timezone(&Utc);\n\n    let duration_seconds = closed_at.signed_duration_since(claimed_at).num_seconds();\n\n    // Update the session with closed_at and duration_seconds\n    let rows_updated = tx.execute(\n        \"UPDATE worker_sessions\n         SET closed_at = ?1, duration_seconds = ?2\n         WHERE bead_id = ?3 AND claimed_at = ?4\",\n        params![\n            closed_at.to_rfc3339(),\n            duration_seconds,\n            bead_id,\n            claimed_at_str\n        ],\n    )?;\n\n    if rows_updated > 0 {\n        // Recompute velocity stats for this (model, harness, issue_type) tuple\n        if let (Some(m), Some(h), Some(it)) = (model, harness, issue_type) {\n            let _ = recompute_velocity_stats(tx, &m, &h, &it);\n        }\n        Ok(true)\n    } else {\n        Ok(false)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Recompute velocity statistics for a given (model, harness, issue_type) tuple.\n///\n/// Queries the last 50 completed sessions for the tuple and computes\n/// p50, p90, and average duration in seconds.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n/// * `model` - The model name\n/// * `harness` - The harness name\n/// * `issue_type` - The issue type\nfn recompute_velocity_stats(\n    tx: &Connection,\n    model: &str,\n    harness: &str,\n    issue_type: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    // Get last 50 completed sessions for this tuple\n    let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n        \"SELECT duration_seconds\n         FROM worker_sessions ws\n         INNER JOIN issues i ON i.id = ws.bead_id\n         WHERE ws.model = ?1\n           AND ws.harness = ?2\n           AND i.issue_type = ?3\n           AND ws.duration_seconds IS NOT NULL\n         ORDER BY ws.closed_at DESC\n         LIMIT 50\",\n    )?;\n\n    let mut durations: Vec<i64> = stmt\n        .query_map(params![model, harness, issue_type], |row| row.get(0))?\n        .collect::<std::result::Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;\n\n    let sample_count = durations.len() as i64;\n    let (p50_seconds, p90_seconds, avg_seconds) = if durations.is_empty() {\n        (None, None, None)\n    } else {\n        durations.sort_unstable();\n        let len = durations.len();\n\n        // Calculate percentiles\n        let p50_idx = (len as f64 * 0.5).floor() as usize;\n        let p90_idx = (len as f64 * 0.9).floor() as usize;\n        let p50 = Some(durations[p50_idx]);\n        let p90 = if len > 1 { Some(durations[p90_idx]) } else { p50 };\n\n        // Calculate average\n        let sum: i64 = durations.iter().sum();\n        let avg = Some(sum as f64 / len as f64);\n\n        (p50, p90, avg)\n    };\n\n    // Upsert into velocity_stats\n    tx.execute(\n        \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO velocity_stats (model, harness, issue_type, sample_count, p50_seconds, p90_seconds, avg_seconds, last_updated)\n         VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, ?7, ?8)\",\n        params![\n            model,\n            harness,\n            issue_type,\n            sample_count,\n            p50_seconds,\n            p90_seconds,\n            avg_seconds,\n            Utc::now().to_rfc3339(),\n        ],\n    )?;\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Get expected duration in seconds for a (model, harness, issue_type) tuple.\n///\n/// Returns the p50 (median) duration if available, otherwise None.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n/// * `model` - The model name (or empty string for unknown)\n/// * `harness` - The harness name (or empty string for unknown)\n/// * `issue_type` - The issue type\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Some(seconds))` - Expected duration in seconds\n/// * `Ok(None)` - No velocity data available for this tuple\npub fn get_expected_seconds(\n    tx: &Connection,\n    model: &str,\n    harness: &str,\n    issue_type: &str,\n) -> Result<Option<i64>> {\n    // Try exact match first\n    let result: Option<i64> = tx.query_row(\n        \"SELECT p50_seconds\n         FROM velocity_stats\n         WHERE model = ?1 AND harness = ?2 AND issue_type = ?3\n           AND sample_count >= 3\",\n        params![model, harness, issue_type],\n        |row| row.get(0),\n    )?;\n\n    if let Some(seconds) = result {\n        return Ok(Some(seconds));\n    }\n\n    // Fallback: try with just model and issue_type (any harness)\n    let result: Option<i64> = tx.query_row(\n        \"SELECT p50_seconds\n         FROM velocity_stats\n         WHERE model = ?1 AND harness = '' AND issue_type = ?3\n           AND sample_count >= 3\",\n        params![model, \"\", issue_type],\n        |row| row.get(0),\n    )?;\n\n    if let Some(seconds) = result {\n        return Ok(Some(seconds));\n    }\n\n    // Fallback: try with just issue_type (any model/harness)\n    let result: Option<i64> = tx.query_row(\n        \"SELECT p50_seconds\n         FROM velocity_stats\n         WHERE model = '' AND harness = '' AND issue_type = ?1\n           AND sample_count >= 10\",\n        params![issue_type],\n        |row| row.get(0),\n    )?;\n\n    Ok(result)\n}\n\n/// Get all velocity statistics.\n///\n/// Returns a list of all (model, harness, issue_type) tuples with their stats.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Vec<VelocityStats>)` - List of velocity stats\npub fn get_all_velocity_stats(tx: &Connection) -> Result<Vec<VelocityStats>> {\n    let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n        \"SELECT model, harness, issue_type, sample_count, p50_seconds, p90_seconds, avg_seconds, last_updated\n         FROM velocity_stats\n         ORDER BY sample_count DESC\",\n    )?;\n\n    let mut stats = Vec::new();\n    let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n        stats.push(VelocityStats {\n            model: row.get(0)?,\n            harness: row.get(1)?,\n            issue_type: row.get(2)?,\n            sample_count: row.get(3)?,\n            p50_seconds: row.get(4)?,\n            p90_seconds: row.get(5)?,\n            avg_seconds: row.get(6)?,\n            last_updated: row.get(7)?,\n        });\n    }\n\n    Ok(stats)\n}\n\n/// Get velocity statistics filtered by model and/or harness.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n/// * `model_filter` - Optional model filter\n/// * `harness_filter` - Optional harness filter\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Vec<VelocityStats>)` - List of velocity stats\npub fn get_velocity_stats(\n    tx: &Connection,\n    model_filter: Option<&str>,\n    harness_filter: Option<&str>,\n) -> Result<Vec<VelocityStats>> {\n    let mut query = String::from(\n        \"SELECT model, harness, issue_type, sample_count, p50_seconds, p90_seconds, avg_seconds, last_updated\n         FROM velocity_stats WHERE 1=1\",\n    );\n\n    let mut params = Vec::new();\n    let mut param_idx = 1;\n\n    if let Some(model) = model_filter {\n        query.push_str(&format!(\" AND model = ?{}\", param_idx));\n        params.push(model.to_string());\n        param_idx += 1;\n    }\n\n    if let Some(harness) = harness_filter {\n        query.push_str(&format!(\" AND harness = ?{}\", param_idx));\n        params.push(harness.to_string());\n        param_idx += 1;\n    }\n\n
